     主席報告
     Chairman’s Statement


     本人欣然向各位股東提呈龍湖集團控股有限公司                                I am pleased to present to our shareholders the half-year business review and
     （（「本公司」），連同其附屬公司，統稱「本集團」）                            outlook for the six months ended June 30, 2025, for Longfor Group Holdings
     二零二五年六月三十日止之半年業務回顧與展                                 Limited (the “Company”, and together with its subsidiaries, collectively the
     望。                                                   “Group”).

     中國經濟在多輪政策協同發力下，正釋放出強勁                                Supported by coordinated policy measures, China’s economy is showing strong
     韌性。地產行業於深度調整中加速重構。面對週                                resilience. The real estate sector is accelerating its restructuring and development
     期考驗，龍湖始終堅守交付與兌付的底線，堅定                                amid significant adjustments. In the face of cyclical challenges, Longfor Group
     調整債務結構、優化資產組合，穩步構建高質量                                has consistently upheld its bottom line of project delivery and debt repayment,
     發展新路徑。                                               firmly adjusting its debt structure, optimizing its asset portfolio, and steadily
                                                          building a new path of high-quality development.

     2025年上半年，龍湖集團實現營業收入588億元                             During the first half of 2025, Longfor Group achieved a total revenue of
     （人民幣，下同），同比增長25%；經營性業務實                              RMB58.8 billion, representing a year-on-year increase of 25%. The revenue
     現收入133億元，同比增長1.3%。期內，本集團                             from investment property operation and property service segments reached
     於全國36城累計交付超100個項目、近4萬套品質                             RMB13.3 billion, representing a year-on-year increase of 1.3%. During the
     房源，持續兌現交付承諾。                                         period, the Group delivered more than 100 projects across 36 cities nationwide,
                                                          providing nearly 40,000 quality housing units, continuously fulfilling its value
                                                          commitment.

     在「加快構建房地產發展新模式」的政策指引下，                               Guided by policies aimed at “accelerating the establishment of a new
     行業正從高速增長轉向高質量發展。公司主動摒                                development model for the real estate industry”, the sector is transitioning from
     棄對槓桿與規模的依賴，聚焦債務壓降與正向現                                rapid growth to high-quality development. The Company has proactively moved
     金流積累。上半年集團有息負債較去年底繼續                                 away from relying on leverage and scale to prioritizing debt reduction and the
     壓降65億元，平均融資成本降至3.58%的歷年低                             accumulation of positive cash flow. By the end of the first half of 2025, the Group
     點，平均合同借貸年期延長至10.95年的歷史高                              reduced its interest-bearing debt by RMB6.5 billion, with the average financing
     位，含資本性支出的經營性現金流實現超20億元                               cost reaching a historical low of 3.58%, and the average contract borrowing
     淨流入，為跨越週期提供堅實支撐。                                     period extended to a historical high of 10.95 years, the operating cash flow,
                                                          including capital expenditures, achieved a net inflow of over RMB2.0 billion,
                                                          providing strong support for navigating through the debt cycle.

     去年9月以來，需求端支持政策為銷售市場注入                                Since September last year, demand-side support policies have injected
     動能，旨在助力實現止跌回穩的目標。當下，集                                momentum into the sales market, helping to stabilize the market after a period
     團開發業務的策略重在平衡存量項目的利潤與現                                of decline. Currently, the Group’s development business strategy focuses on
     金流，全力攻堅去化、回籠資金；新貨源拓展則                                balancing profits and cash flow from existing projects while striving to accelerate
     堅持審慎投資策略。以客戶需求為導向提升產品                                sales and recover funds. For new project expansions, a prudent investment
     力－交付好房子、提供好服務，始終是開發業務                                strategy is in place. Enhancing product strength by focusing on customer needs,
     的核心邏輯。                                               delivering quality homes and providing high-quality services, remains the core
                                                          principal of the development business.

能力與現金流反哺能力不斷增強。            contributed steadily, with its sustainable profitability and cash flow generation
                           capabilities becoming increasingly strengthening.

龍湖在營的89座商場（包含75座重資產及14座輕   Longfor’s 89 shopping malls in operation (including 75 heavy assets and 14
資產）表現亮眼：上半年出租率保持97%高位，     light assets) delivered outstanding performance: in the first half of the year, the
整體銷售額增幅約17%，日均客流增幅約11%。    occupancy rate remained high at 97%, while total sales rose by approximately
聚焦一二線高能級城市的佈局策略，有效鎖定高      17% and average daily customer traffic rose by approximately 11%. The strategic
消費力客群，既保障穩定客流，更提升商業綜合      focus on first- and second-tier cities effectively targets high-spending customer
體的長期價值。運營端通過「一店一策」的差異化     groups, ensuring stable footfall and enhancing the long-term value of commercial
打造，持續輸出獨有的創新消費場景與體驗。       complexes. On the operational side, a differentiated “one store, one strategy”
                           approach continuously delivers unique and innovative consumer scenarios and
                           experiences.

資管航道在長租公寓、產業辦公、服務式公寓、      The asset management segment has made coordinated efforts across six business

     回顧及展望                                                REVIEW AND OUTLOOK
     上半年，在政策支持及需求結構調整的背景下，                                In the first half of the year, supported by policy measures and structural
     房地產市場延續修復態勢，全國商品房銷售額實                                adjustments in demand, China’s property market continued to recover. The
     現4.4萬億人民幣，同比下降5.5%。年初以來，                             national new home sales reached RMB4.4 trillion, representing a year-on-year
     政策端持續發力，圍繞穩預期、促需求、優供                                 decline of 5.5%. Since the beginning of the year, regulators have introduced
     給、降風險等方面出台多項措施，推動房地產市                                a series of initiatives aimed at stabilizing expectations, stimulating demand,
     場止跌回穩。                                               optimizing supply, and mitigating risks, which collectively helped to stem the
                                                          downturn and restore stability in the property market.

     集團堅持構建高質量發展模式，聚焦開發業務、                                Based on the high-quality development model, the Group centers on three
     運營業務、服務業務三大業務板塊，堅定地產開                                business segments of property development, investment property operation
     發、商業投資、資產管理、物業管理、智慧營造                                and property service and firmly pursue the synergies between five major
     五大業務航道的協同發展。集團始終以客戶需求                                business divisions such as property development, commercial investment,
     為導向，持續迭代組織體系，強化行活、提質增                                asset management, property management, and smart construction. Guided by
     效。                                                   customer needs, the Group continuously iterates and upgrades its organizational
                                                          structure to enhance routine practices, operational efficiency and quality.

     開發業務方面，上半年實現合約銷售金額350.1                              In terms of property development, the Group achieved contracted sales of
     億人民幣，其中一二線城市銷售佔比約90%，並                               RMB35.01 billion during the first half of the year, with tier-1 and tier-2 cities
     表回款率超100%，保持高質量回款。下半年集                               contributing around 90%. The consolidated cash collection ratio exceeded 100%,
     團將繼續堅持在核心城市的投資佈局，結合市場                                continuing to maintain high-quality cash collection. In the second half of the
     情況靈活鋪排推盤節奏，聚焦存貨去化，並以客                                year, the Group will continue to strengthen its investment presence in core cities,
     戶為中心持續精進產品力。                                         flexibly launch saleable resources with consideration of market situations, focus
                                                          on inventory reduction, and further enhance product competitiveness through a
                                                          customer-centric approach.

     商業投資方面，上半年如期新增運營1座重資產                                Regarding commercial investment in the first half of the year, the Group opened
     商場。截至年中，龍湖商業累計運營89座商場，                               an asset-heavy shopping mall as scheduled. As of 30 June, Longfor Commercial
     期末出租率保持97%。龍湖商業持續推動存量項                               has 89 shopping malls in operation with an occupancy rate of 97%. Moreover, the
     目的改造升級、煥新消費體驗、提升經營表現，                                Group continued to renovate and enhance its existing projects, enrich consumer
     上半年租金收入同比增長4.9%至55.0億人民幣。                            experiences, and improve operational performance. Rental income for the period

     企業管治                                                 CORPORATE GOVERNANCE
     本公司知悉公司透明度及問責十分重要，致力於                                The Company recognises the importance of corporate transparency and
     達致高水準的企業管治及通過更有效的企業管治                                accountability. We are committed in achieving a high standard of corporate
     帶領本集團取得更好業績及提升公司價值。於截                                governance and leading the Group to attain better results and enhance company
     至二零二五年六月三十日止六個月，本公司一直                                value with effective corporate governance procedures. During the six months
     採用、應用及遵守上市規則附錄C1第二部分所                                ended June 30, 2025, the Company has adopted, applied and complied with
     載的企業管治守則（「守則」）所載守則條文。惟以                              the code provisions as set out in the Corporate Governance Code (the “Code”)
     下偏離除外：                                               contained in Part 2 of Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules, except with the
                                                          following deviation:

     於二零二二年十月二十八日委任陳序平先生為董                                Following the appointment of Mr. Chen Xuping as the Chairman of the Board
     事會主席生效後，陳序平先生身兼本公司董事                                 with effect from October 28, 2022, Mr. Chen Xuping assumes the dual roles of
     會主席及首席執行官兩職。是項委任與守則第                                 the Chairman of the Board and the Chief Executive Officer of the Company. This
     C.2.1守則條文有所偏離，其規定主席與行政總                              deviates from code provision C.2.1 of the Code, which requires that the roles
     裁之角色應有區分，並不應由一人同時兼任。於                                of chairman and the chief executive officer should be separate and should not
     評估本集團之發展情況並考慮陳序平先生之經                                 be performed by the same individual. After evaluating the development of the
     驗，董事會認為本公司董事會主席及首席執行官                                Group and taking into account of the experience of Mr. Chen Xuping, the Board
     由一人同時兼任於現階段符合本公司之最佳利                                 was of the opinion that it is in the best interest of the Company at the present
     益，有利於執行本集團之發展戰略。惟董事會將                                stage for vesting the roles of the Chairman of the Board and the Chief Executive
     不時檢討此架構並將於適當時候考慮將兩個職位                                Officer of the Company in the same person as it helps to facilitate the execution
     分開。                                                  of the Group’s development strategies. The Board will nevertheless review this
                                                          structure from time to time and will consider the segregation of the two roles at
                                                          the appropriate time.

     報表的工作包括主要向負責財務與會計事務的人                                Certified Public Accountants. A review of these condensed consolidated financial
     員作出查詢，進行分析以及其他審閱程序。審閱                                statements consists of making inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for
     的範圍遠小於根據香港審計準則進行審核的範                                 financial and accounting matters, and applying analytical and other review
     圍，因此無法確保吾等可知悉在審核中可能發現                                procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an audit conducted in
     的所有重大事項，故吾等並無發表審核意見。                                 accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing and consequently does not
                                                          enable us to obtain assurance that we would become aware of all significant
                                                          matters that might be identified in an audit. Accordingly, we do not express an
                                                          audit opinion.

        附註：                              Notes:


        (a)   本集團有權於有權調整票面利率的            (a)      The Group is entitled to adjust the coupon rate on the date of entitlement
              日期調整票面利率及投資者可選擇                     to adjust the coupon rate and the investors have the option to sell back the
              於有權調整票面利率的日期至投資                     bonds between the date of entitlement to adjust the coupon rate and the date
              者行使選擇權的日期間按面值回售                     of investors to exercise the option at par or hold until maturity.
              債券或持有至到期。


        (b)   債券於期內悉數贖回。                 (b)      The bond has been fully redeemed during the period.


        (c)   該債券已由投資者行使提早贖回選            (c)      The bond has been exercised the early redemption option by the investors

            於報告期末，本集團有指定為高效對沖工                               At the end of the reporting period, the Group had cross currency interest
            具的交叉貨幣利率掉期，以透過將一定比                               rate swaps designated as highly effective hedging instruments in order
            例的定息美元優先票據及相應利息付款由                               to minimise its exposures to foreign currency and cash flow interest
            港元及美元轉換為人民幣來盡量減低本集                               rate risk on its fixed rate USD senior notes and corresponding interest
            團承受的該等借款及優先票據及相應利息                               payment by swapping a portion of those borrowings and senior notes and
            付款的外幣及現金流量利率風險。                                  corresponding interest payment from HKD and USD to RMB.

            於報告期末，本集團有指定為有效對沖工                               At the end of reporting period, the Group had foreign currency forward
            具的外匯遠期合約及封頂封底遠期合約，                               contracts and CFF contracts designated as effective hedging instruments in
            以通過於借款到期日以人民幣兌換港元來                               order to minimise foreign currency risk on its HKD bank borrowings by
            盡量減低其港元銀行借貸的外匯風險。                                selling RMB in exchange for HKD at the borrowings’ maturity dates.

            交叉貨幣利率掉期、外匯遠期合約及封頂                               The terms of the cross currency interest rate swaps, foreign currency
            封底遠期合約的條款經已磋商以配合各項                               forward contracts and CFF contracts have been negotiated to match the
            指定對沖項目的條款，而董事認為該等交                               terms of the respective designated hedged items and the directors consider
            叉貨幣利率掉期、外匯遠期合約及封頂封                               that the cross currency interest rate swaps, foreign currency forward
            底遠期合約屬有效對沖工具。                                    contracts and CFF contracts are effective hedging instruments.

25. 或然負債                       25. CONTINGENT LIABILITIES
  於二零二五年六月三十日，本集團就本集              The Group provided guarantees amounting to RMB12,718,226,000 (at
  團物業買方獲授按揭銀行貸款提供人民               December 31, 2024: RMB14,713,009,000) as at June 30, 2025 in respect
  幣12,718,226,000元（於二零二四年十二月      of mortgage bank loans granted to purchasers of the Group’s properties.
  三十一日：人民幣14,713,009,000元）的擔      Guarantees are given to banks with respect to loans procured by the
  保。本集團就本集團物業買方之銀行貸款              purchasers of the Group’s properties. Such guarantees will be released by
  向銀行提供擔保。該等擔保將於向買方交              banks upon delivery of the properties to the purchasers and completion of
  收物業及相關按揭物業登記完成時由銀行              the relevant mortgage properties registration.
  解除。


  本公司董事認為，本集團物業買方的該等              In the opinion of the directors of the Company, the fair values of these
  財務擔保合約的公平值於初始確認時並不              financial guarantee contracts to the purchasers of the Group’s properties
  重大，以及本公司董事認為向客戶提供的              are insignificant at initial recognition and the directors of the Company
  財務擔保的信貸風險極低，原因為買方之              consider that the credit risk exposure to financial guarantees provided
  過往可觀察違約比率較低及融資以物業作              to customers are remote because the historical observed default ratio by
  抵押且物業的市價高於擔保金額，因此，              purchasers is relatively low and the facilities are secured by the properties
  於二零二五年六月三十日及二零二四年               and the market price of the properties is higher than the guaranteed
  十二月三十一日的報告期末因擔保合約產              amounts, accordingly, the expected credit loss arising from the guarantee
  生的預期信貸虧損被認為不重大。                 contracts is considered to be insignificant at the end of the reporting period
                                  as at June 30, 2025 and December 31, 2024.

26. 以股份為基礎的付款交易                26. SHARE-BASED PAYMENT TRANSACTIONS
  本公司採納限制性股份激勵計劃。限制性              Restricted share award scheme were adopted by the Company. The
  股份激勵計劃的目的在於認可及激勵本               objective of the restricted share award scheme is to recognise and motivate
  集團董事及僱員作出的貢獻，提供激勵，              the contribution of directors and employees of the Group, to provide
  幫助本集團挽留其現有僱員及招募額外僱              incentives, to help the Group in retaining its existing employees and
  員，並為彼等提供經濟利益，以實現本集              recruiting additional employees and to provide them with economic
  團的長期業務目標。股份激勵計劃容許本              interests in attaining the long-term business objectives of the Group. The
  集團向合資格人士派發由一名獨立受託人              share award scheme allows the Group to make bonus payments to eligible
  購入及持有（直至指定的歸屬條件達成為              persons by way of the Company’s shares acquired by and held through
  止）的本公司股份作為花紅。                   an independent trustee until fulfillment of specified conditions before
                                  vesting.

  於二零二五年五月三十日，本公司根據受              On May 30, 2025, the Company awarded 53,930,000 shares to the
  限制股份激勵計劃將53,930,000股股份授         directors and employees of the Group under the restricted share award
  予本集團董事及僱員。授予董事及僱員的              scheme. The share awards granted to directors and employees are to be
  股份激勵將於二十年以上歸屬期間各年按              vested in equal entitlement during each year of the vesting period over
  同等權利歸屬。於二零二五年五月三十日              20 years respectively. The first vesting date of the share awards granted
  授出之股份激勵的第一個歸屬日期為二零              on May 30, 2025 is May 30, 2026. The estimated fair value of the awards
  二六年五月三十日。已授出激勵的估計公              granted is RMB 485,823,000.
  平值為人民幣485,823,000元。

  按經常性基準以公平值計量的本                Fair value of the Group’s financial assets and liabilities that are

  本集團部分金融資產及負債於各報告期末            Some of the Group’s financial assets and liabilities are measured at fair
  按公平值計量。下表提供如何計量該等             value at the end of each reporting period. The following table gives
  金融資產及負債之公平值（特別是所使用            information about how the fair values of these financial assets and
  之估值技術及輸入數據），及根據公平值            liabilities are determined (in particular, the valuation technique(s) and
  計量輸入數據之可觀測程度所劃分之公平            inputs used), as well as the level of the fair value hierarchy into which the
  值計量之公平值級別（第一至三層級）之資           fair value measurements are categorised (levels 1 to 3) based on the degree
  料。                            to which the inputs to the fair value measurements is observable.

  •    第一層級公平值計量是按活躍市場          •     Level 1 fair value measurements are those derived from quoted
       上相同資產或負債報價（不作調整）               prices (unadjusted) in active market for identical assets or liabilities;
       得出之公平值計量；


  •    第二層級公平值計量是指第一層級          •     Level 2 fair value measurements are those derived from inputs other
       之報價以外，可直接（即價格）或間               than quoted prices included within Level 1 that are observable for
       接（即由價格得出）觀察之資產或負               the asset or liability, either directly (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (i.e.
       債得出之公平值計量；及                    derived from prices); and

  •    第三層級公平值計量是指以市場不          •     Level 3 fair value measurements are those derived from valuation
       可觀察之數據為依據，作資產或負                techniques that include inputs for the asset or liability that are not
       債之相關輸入數據（不可觀察輸入                based on observable market data (unobservable inputs).
       數據），以估值技術得出之公平值
       計量。

                                                                                                    上市權益工具的公平值由活躍市場之買入
                                                                                                    價報價釐定，而非上市權益工具的公平值
                                                                                                    由實體本身證券的近期交易釐定，或按可
                                                                                                    比公司的價格╱收益計算，並對相關投資
                                                                                                    組合就缺乏市場流動性作出折現調整及對
                                                                                                    相關開支作出調整。
               Unlisted equity instruments designated at   3,291,873        3,966,559      Level 3 The fair value of equity instruments
               FVTOCI                                                                              designated as at FVTOCI are determined
                                                                                                   by the net asset value of the private equity
                                                                                                   fund with underlying assets and liabilities
                                                                                                   measured at fair value. The fair value of
                                                                                                   listed equity instruments are determined
                                                                                                   by quoted bid prices in an active market
                                                                                                   while the unlisted equity instruments are
                                                                                                   determined by recent transaction of the

                                                                                                   expenses.
